{"id": "e30ecd052ecf8936", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-26T19:55:52.767060+00:00", "author": "max4", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "Full-objective transfer retaining the initial-distance term: at the Huber level and FIXED optimum-flow vector mu, all affine certificate changes are exactly T e=T^T e=T pi=0 and are spanned by directed rank-one moves. Their exact PSD test is on the full Schur remainder with every kernel case retained. A physical unit-step backward-skip elimination preserves every optimum coefficient and deletes its target in one exact feasible move. No global termination or growing-horizon certificate is claimed.", "evidence": "# A precise transfer of directed moves to full objective certificates\n\nStatus: candidate, not verifier-accepted. This records a usable interface to\nthe public C51 full-objective object047c257795dc59ba without assuming that\nunverified finite object as a theorem. It supplies no growing-horizon\ncertificate and no new sign-descent/termination claim. The full initial\ndistance is retained throughout. No numerical or verifier call.\n\nLet h be ANY finite nonnegative schedule of length n>=1 and positive sum\nS. Put q=1/(2S+1), P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, and use the formal vectors\n(x0,g0,...,gn), positions xi=x0-sum_(t<i)h_t gt, and the original\nQij=fi-fj+<gi,xj-xi>+||gi-gj||^2/2. The optimum jet is zero.\nAssume an actual nonnegative full certificate is supplied:\n\n q||x0||^2-2fn+sum lambda_ij Qij >=0\n\nas a FULL formal quadratic identity after all value coefficients cancel.\nThe assumption includes all original zero-step gradients separately.\n\nFirst, this certificate has lambda_(star,i)=0. To prove it directly, use\nthe scalar fixed Huber function with threshold q and initial point1.\nIts samples are xi=1-qP_i, gi=q, fi=qxi-q^2/2. They all lie on the\npositive affine ray since P_i<=S, and 2fn=q. Every finite-source\ninterpolation residual (including target star) is zero, whereas\nQ_(star,i)=-q[1-q(P_i+1)]<0 because S>0. Thus full PSD and lambda>=0\nforce all star-source multipliers to vanish. The full matrix also\nannihilates v_H=(1,q,...,q), since its nonnegative quadratic is zero\nthere. This step is an explicit legal-trajectory calculation; interpolation\nnecessity and normalization are available from fact1252b8823bb5a290.\n\nWrite mu_i=lambda_(i,star). Finite value flow gives sum mu_i=2.\nThe matrix of the full certificate has blocks\n\n M_full=[q,-mu^T/2; -mu/2,B_full],\n Z=B_full-mu mu^T/(4q),\n M_full=q||x0-sum_i mu_i g_i/(2q)||^2+g^T Z g.        (1)\n\nThe identity is obtained simply by completing the x0 square. Hence the\nFULL certificate is PSD exactly when Z>=0; v_H in the kernel gives Ze=0.\nThe initial-distance square and every optimum coefficient remain explicit.\n\nLet L be the row Laplacian of the FINITE lambda entries. Consider changes\npreserving q and every mu_i. Denote the change of L by T_mat. The exact\nlinear conditions preserving every value flow and the Huber kernel are\n\n T_mat e=0, T_mat^T e=0, T_mat pi=0,                 (2)\n\nwhere A_mat has diagonal1 and lower-column entries h_t, and pi=A_mat e=e+P.\nThe first condition is automatic for row Laplacians, the second follows\nfrom the fixed finite value flows, and the third is the kernel condition.\nTo verify the last assertion, the gradient part of Qij can be written as\n<gi,(A_mat g)_i-(A_mat g)_j>+(||gj||^2-||gi||^2)/2.\nConsequently the full change is\n\n delta M_full=[0,0;0,sym(T_mat A_mat)],\n delta Z=sym(T_mat A_mat).                           (3)\n\nThe possible diagonal term from the final gradient norms is zero because\nT_mat^T e=0. Thus delta Z e=T_mat pi/2. Equations(2) are necessary for\nthe difference of two supplied full certificates with the same q,mu;\nconversely they preserve every stated affine condition. Actual finite\ncoefficient signs and full Z PSD still need their separate checks.\n\nThe rank-one directions are particularly explicit:\n\n T_mat=t u v^T, e^T u=0, v^T e=v^T pi=0,\n z_vec=A_mat^T v,\n delta lambda_ij=-t u_i v_j (finite i!=j),\n delta Z=t sym(u z_vec^T).                           (4)\n\nEvery optimum multiplier remains unchanged. The EXACT sign interval is\nthe intersection of lambda_ij-t u_i v_j>=0 over ALL ordered finite pairs,\nincluding the terminal source and target. The exact full PSD interval is\nthat of Z+t sym(u z_vec^T), including the true kernel of Z, not an\nassumed positive-definite quotient. Completing the square in(1) proves\nthat no initial-vector direction is lost by this test.\n\nFor clarity a self-contained kernel-aware test is available. Split u,z_vec\ninto projections to Range(Z) and Ker(Z). If both kernel projections are\nzero, whiten on Range(Z); with a=u^T Z^+u, b=z_vec^T Z^+z_vec,\nc=u^T Z^+z_vec and d=sqrt(ab), the interval is\n[-2/(c+d),2/(d-c)], removing any bound with denominator zero. This\nfollows from the two nonzero update eigenvalues (c+d)/2,(c-d)/2.\nIf exactly one kernel projection is zero and neither whole vector is\nzero, no nonzero t is legal: its zero kernel diagonal forces its mixed\nrow to vanish. Noncollinear nonzero kernel projections also prohibit\nnonzero t, by a negative two-dimensional determinant. If z_N=k u_N\nwith both nonzero, k!=0, the exact nonzero-t conditions are\n\n tk>0, (t/(4k))(k u_R-z_R)^T Z^+(k u_R-z_R)<=1.       (5)\n\nIndeed the kernel block is tk u_Nu_N^T; completing that one square\nleaves Z_R-(t/(4k))(k u_R-z_R)(k u_R-z_R)^T. Whiten Z_R to obtain(5).\nA zero whole u or z_vec gives no matrix change and all t pass PSD.\nEvery case is therefore stated, including legal changes of kernel.\n\nThe rank-one family also spans ALL the linear changes(2), though this\ndoes not prove they can be taken sequentially while feasible. Since S>0,\ne and pi are independent. Choose matrices E_basis,V_basis whose columns\nare bases for e-perp and span(e,pi)-perp respectively. Every T_mat in(2)\nhas the form E_basis C V_basis^T. Expand C by its individual entries to\nwrite T_mat as a sum of directions uv^T satisfying(4). The linear\nspace has dimension n(n-1). This span argument implies no admissible\npath or termination result; singular PSD faces and multiplier zeros can\nblock individual terms of a decomposition.\n\nThere is a concrete elimination which preserves the full initial-distance\ncertificate for arbitrary length. Suppose two consecutive physical steps\nare h_j=1 and h_(j+1)=b>0. On queries j,j+1,j+2 take\nv=(b,-1-b,1), u=A_mat^T v=(0,-1,1), extended by zero. Both scalar\nmoment constraints in(4) hold in the ORIGINAL global indices. The exact\ninterpolation identity is\n\n b Q_(j+1,j)+Q_(j+1,j+2)+(1+b)Q_(j+2,j+1)\n                   -b Q_(j+2,j)=||g_(j+2)-g_(j+1)||^2. (6)\n\nNo optimum residual is changed. On 0<=t<=lambda_(j+2,j)/b, adding\nt times(6) preserves every actual sign and the full PSD certificate,\nand the upper endpoint deletes the backward skip j+2->j. It is the\nonly decreasing actual coefficient in(6). Thus this is a genuine full\nobjective move, with the initial-distance term intact, for supplied\ncertificates satisfying this physical unit-step hypothesis. It is not\na claim that the assigned critical family has unit steps.\n\nFor general adjacent positive a,b, the same choice\nv=(b,-a-b,a), u=(b(1-a),-a-b(1-a),a) produces the full square\nt||b(1-a)g_j-[a+b(1-a)]g_(j+1)+a g_(j+2)||^2.\nEvery actual donor must be retained: the exact nonnegative upper\nendpoint is min lambda_ij/(u_i v_j) over u_i v_j>0. This version\napplies to long-step neighborhoods without the unit-step hypothesis,\nbut is useful only if its required donors are present.\n\nScope for the active direct-upper branch: these are complete fixed-mu\ndirections for full Huber-level certificates, with actual signs and\nfull PSD checked by(1)--(5). They can be applied conditionally to any\nsupplied feasible C51-type array and do not require an intrinsic\nprimitive certificate. A method preserving mu cannot choose a different\noptimum-flow vector; bulk re-selection across that boundary needs\nadditional directions. No general growing-horizon choice, feasibility\ncontinuation, or objective/profile improvement is proved here.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"guidance_id": "6f0f42aa11644848", "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "public_object_considered": "047c257795dc59ba", "finding_ids": ["dee433e21acc29dc", "baaa3c7d05bec3d7", "e20fa4c2bad75795"], "artifact": "local_memory/full_objective_fixed_mu_moves.md", "status": "candidate_only", "scope": "Fixed optimum-flow fiber only; signed intervals must be checked on all finite edges; no intrinsic reduction imposed.", "solver_calls_new": 0, "verifier_calls_new": 0}, "glossary": {"mu": "Vector of optimum-target multipliers lambda_(i,star), fixed by the proposed moves.", "q": "Huber objective level1/(2sum h+1), scoped to this finding.", "M_full": "Matrix of the full formal objective certificate in (x0,g0,...,gn).", "B_full": "Gradient-gradient block of M_full.", "Z": "Full gradient Schur remainder B_full-mu mu^T/(4q).", "T_mat": "Change of the row Laplacian of the finite multiplier array.", "A_mat": "GD trajectory matrix with diagonal1 and lower-column entries h_t.", "pi": "A_mat e, the vector1+P_i of prefix capacities.", "v_H": "Full Huber kernel vector(1,q,...,q).", "z_vec": "A_mat^T v in a directed rank-one move.", "E_basis": "Any column basis for e-perp.", "V_basis": "Any column basis for span(e,pi)-perp.", "Z_plus": "Inverse of Z on its range and zero on its kernel, written Z^+."}}
{"id": "694fb2dacb4f7389", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-26T19:57:33.616025+00:00", "author": "max3", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "C53 key joint derivation: mixed boundary terms cannot repair ANY repeated-copy join in max's (18) family, even with nongreedy storage, all retained child remainders, or increasing the final q. At the one-step seed choose both formal gradients=e; direct star-pattern expansion gives E_1=(k_1^2-Z_1)||e||^2. Inductively use right template terminal e and left template terminal g=-k_j e/d_j. Then V=d_j g+z_right=0 and z_parent=k_j g=k_(j+1)e. The FULL join E_new=E_left+rE_right+t||V||^2+2<V,(H-ell)g+ell e>+(Z-rd^2)||g||^2+(rZ-Znew)||e||^2 evaluates exactly to (k_(j+1)^2-Znew)||e||^2: all r,Z_child,H,ell,t cancel. This induction does not require child E PSD, zero template energy, or maximal Z choices. For the objective matrix q||x0||^2-2f_n+sum 2lambda_ki Q_ik/v, set template gradients with terminal e and let x0 vary freely. Multiplying by v>0 gives the 2x2 principal restriction [[qv,-b],[-b,2k_m b-v]], where b=k_m+u-1 and Z=(u-1)^2+v. PSD forces q>=b^2/[v(2k_m b-v)]>=1/k_m^2 because (v-k_m b)^2>=0. For epsilon=1/m,c=lambda(1+epsilon),a=(1-epsilon)/c,d_j=a c^j, let R=(2+a)/(c(1-epsilon)). Exact denominator subtraction gives 1-R=epsilon[2-lambda^2 epsilon(1+epsilon)]/[lambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)]>=epsilon/4 for m>=128. Thus |k_m|=(1-epsilon)c^m R^(2^(m-1))<c^m exp[-2^(m-3)/m]. This is a formal certificate obstruction, not a legal primal witness. The required two-variable/full-PSD proof includes actual array/flow expansion and will be published in full; no predecessor beyond accepted1252 is used as a mathematical brick.", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"branch_id": "C53_full_boundary_repair", "finding_ids": ["c7f10ed5dc99275b", "09f0b530f2583c54"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "status": "joint derivation, unverified"}, "glossary": {"z_j": "Within C53, weighted sum of all queried gradients plus the terminal gradient of block H_j.", "k_j": "Within C53, signed moment of the formal recursive template with terminal gradient1; k_1=2+a and k_(j+1)=-k_j^2/d_j.", "kappa_m": "Within C53, absolute value of the signed template coefficient k_m.", "Z_j": "Within C53, endpoint storage (u_j-1)^2+v_j for the displayed block potential.", "R": "Within C53 parameter estimate, ratio (2+a)/(c(1-epsilon)); not an error functional."}}
{"id": "2105aae5913d003a", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-26T20:06:40.779883+00:00", "author": "max", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "Independent-history direct-upper attempt now has an actual FOUR-FAN recursive multiplier formula with all value and full Gram coefficients, and a whole-class failure: at Huber level, a child with nonproportional queried proof weights forces u/gamma<=S/(3M+2d-1), but the full first-gradient diagonal requires u/gamma>a/(1+a). They conflict on the ACTUAL critical words for m>=64. A completely specified midpoint-only subrule fails at EVERY q, including after an arbitrary final-step replacement, without a moment-rank assumption. This is an array obstruction, not a schedule lower witness.", "evidence": "# Independent proof weights: an exact join and a full-matrix obstruction\n\nStatus: joint, self-contained UNVERIFIED candidate. No solver and no\nfact_submit. This is a failure of explicitly specified multiplier families,\nnot a lower witness for the schedules and not a resolution of the target.\n\nThe accepted mathematical input is the interpolation inequality from\nfact `1252b8823bb5a290`. The weighted-history approach was informed by\nGrimmer--Shu--Wang, *Composing Optimized Stepsize Schedules for Gradient\nDescent*, arXiv:2410.16249, Proposition 4 and its proof, and by the public\nfinite candidate C51 (047c257795dc59ba). Neither unverified C51 nor an\nexternal composability theorem is used as a proof premise. In particular,\nno quadratic-product equality or primitive hypothesis is imposed here.\n\n## 1. Full objective certificate with independent proof weights\n\nNormalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For distinct sampled indices use\n\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0,\n    C_ij=-2Q_ji\n        =2(f_i-f_j)+2<g_j,x_j-x_i>-||g_i-g_j||^2 >=0.\n\nSet C_ii=0. All positions are the actual GD substitutions. The formal\nvectors x_0,g_0,...,g_n remain independent.\n\nFor a block h of length n, write S=sum h_i, P=x_start-x_end=sum h_i g_i.\nChoose nonnegative proof weights w_0,...,w_n with D=sum w_i>0, and define\n\n    W=sum_i w_i g_i,\n    J_w=2sum_i w_i(f_i-f_end)+sum_i w_i||g_i||^2\n          +2sum_i w_i<g_i,x_start-x_i>-||W||^2.\n\nThe exact identity, retaining the initial distance, is\n\n    ||x_start||^2-2D f_end+2sum_i w_i Q_(i,star)\n        =||x_start-W||^2+J_w.                         (1)\n\nSuppose a finite-edge array lambda_ij>=0 gives\n\n    J_w=sum_(finite i!=j) lambda_ij C_ij+R.            (2)\n\nIts necessary and sufficient value cancellation is\n\n    out_i-in_i=w_i-D 1_(i=end),                       (3)\n\nwhere out_i=sum_j lambda_ij and in_i=sum_j lambda_ji.\nFor every real q the resulting complete full objective identity is\n\n    q||x_start||^2-2f_end\n      +2/D sum_i w_i Q_(i,star)\n      +2/D sum_(finite i!=j) lambda_ij Q_ji\n       =(q-1/D)||x_start||^2\n          +[||x_start-W||^2+R]/D.                    (4)\n\nEvery multiplier in (4) is nonnegative. At q=1/D, its full PSD condition\nis exactly R>=0, since x_start can be set equal to W in the formal space.\nThis formulation places no equality between the proof weights and h.\n\nWhen D=2S+1, all finite gradients equal to one give J_w=0 after using\nf_i-f_end=S-P_i, where P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t. These are legal Huber jets\nwith x_start=D and x_i=D-P_i. Every finite C_ij is zero. Consequently\nR(1,...,1)=0, and if R is PSD then R annihilates the all-ones vector.\n\n## 2. A first-gradient condition for EVERY such full certificate\n\nAssume n>=1 and h_0=1+a with a>0. Let e_0 mean the formal assignment\ng_0=1 and every other block gradient zero. All finite positions after\nthe first step have the same displacement h_0 from the start. Directly\nfrom (2)--(3),\n\n    R(e_0)=w_0-w_0^2+out_0-(2h_0-1)in_0\n          =2w_0-w_0^2-2a in_0.                      (5)\n\nThus the g_0 diagonal of the full unscaled matrix\n||x_start-W||^2+R at x_start=0 is\n\n    2w_0-2a in_0.                                   (6)\n\nThis calculation uses all finite edges, without a support assumption.\nIn particular any PSD certificate needs in_0<=w_0/a.\n\nAlso every PSD certificate in this form has w_0>0. Indeed, if w_0=0,\n(5) and PSD force in_0=out_0=0. Hence no finite edge touches index 0.\nEvery remaining finite position difference cancels the term h_0 g_0.\nIn J_w, however, the g_0 cross coefficient with g_i is 2h_0 w_i for\ni>0. At least one such w_i is positive because D>0. R would then have\na zero g_0 diagonal and a nonzero g_0 row, impossible for a PSD matrix:\nthe two-variable restriction 2bxy+cy^2 with b!=0 takes negative values.\n\n## 3. The complete four-fan join, with both moments retained\n\nTake two certificates (2) on the same physical block h, with proof\nweights w^L,w^R, each of mass D=2S+1; the two arrays and remainders\nmay differ. Let p=n be the left endpoint and q=2n+1 the parent endpoint.\nInsert the actual bridge b=1+d, d>=0, and denote\n\n    M=S+1, T=M+d, M'=2M+d, S'=M'-1, D'=2M'-1,\n    g=g_p, e=g_q,\n    P_L=sum_(left queried i) h_i g_i,\n    P_R=sum_(right queried i) h_i g_i,\n    W_L=sum_(left samples i) w^L_i g_i,\n    W_R=sum_(right samples i) w^R_i g_i.\n\nChoose nonnegative u,r,A,B,C,E and real k,l. The proposed parent weights\nare u w^L on the left, r w^R on the right, with k added at p and l at q.\nRequire every FINAL parent weight to be nonnegative and\n\n    (u+r)D+k+l=D'.                                  (7)\n\nSet gamma_L=u+C+E and gamma_R=r+A+B. Embed gamma_L times the entire\nleft finite array and gamma_R times the entire right finite array.\nAdd the following nonnegative endpoint fans, omitting self edges:\n\n    C w^L_i on (p,i),    E w^L_i on (q,i),   i in left samples,\n    A w^R_i on (p,i),    B w^R_i on (q,i),   i in right samples.\n\nFinally add p0 on (p,q) and q0 on (q,p), where p0,q0>=0 and\n\n    p0-q0=(u-A+E)D+k.                               (8)\n\nThese instructions specify actual finite multipliers, not merely a\ngradient-only matrix with unchecked coefficient signs. All old edges\ninside each copy remain. Define W'=u W_L+r W_R+k g+l e. The exact\nparent identity has remainder\n\n    R'=gamma_L R_L+gamma_R R_R+F,                    (9)\n\nwhere the COMPLETE local quadratic is\n\n    F=gamma_L||W_L||^2+gamma_R||W_R||^2-||W'||^2\n       +2r<W_R,P_L+b g>\n       +k||g||^2+2k<g,P_L>\n       +l||e||^2+2l<e,P_L+b g+P_R>\n       -2(C+E)<W_L,P_L>-2C<W_L,g>\n       -2E<W_L,b g+P_R+e>\n       +(A+C)D||g||^2+(B+E)D||e||^2\n       +2A d<g,W_R>-2B<W_R,P_R+e>\n       +p0[||g||^2+||e||^2+2d<g,e>+2<e,P_R>]\n       +q0[-(2b-1)||g||^2+||e||^2-2<g,e>-2<g,P_R>]. (10)\n\nHere P_L and W_L are genuinely distinct; none of their cross terms has\nbeen discarded. To verify (9)--(10), the four fan identities are\n\n    sum_L w_i C_(p,i)\n       =-J_L-||W_L||^2+2<W_L,P_L+g>-D||g||^2,\n    sum_L w_i C_(q,i)\n       =-J_L-||W_L||^2+2<W_L,P_L+b g+P_R+e>\n                                            -D||e||^2-2D(f_p-f_q),\n    sum_R w_i C_(p,i)\n       =2D(f_p-f_q)-J_R-||W_R||^2\n                                            -2d<g,W_R>-D||g||^2,\n    sum_R w_i C_(q,i)\n       =-J_R-||W_R||^2+2<W_R,P_R+e>-D||e||^2.       (11)\n\nExpanding J of the concatenated weights and subtracting (11) leaves\nthe value coefficient 2[(u-A+E)D+k-p0+q0](f_p-f_q), which is zero\nby (8), and exactly (10). This proves every value and Gram coefficient.\n\n## 4. A whole-family obstruction when the left moments are independent\n\nAssume the two child remainders are PSD. Suppose that the left queried\nweight vector (w^L_0,...,w^L_(n-1)) is not proportional to the physical\nstep vector (h_0,...,h_(n-1)). Then the three formal linear forms\n(P_L,W_L,g) are independent. The terminal gradient is an independent\ncoordinate, and the two queried coefficient rows are linearly independent.\n\nIf (9) were PSD, it would annihilate the all-ones gradient vector, as\nwould both child remainders. Hence F must be stationary at\n\n    P_L=P_R=S, W_L=W_R=D, g=e=1.\n\nThe P_L derivative in (10), divided by 2, is\n\n    rD+k+l-(C+E)D=D'-gamma_L D.\n\nThe W_L derivative, divided by 2, is\n\n    gamma_L D-uD'-CM-EM'.\n\nIndependence of P_L,W_L,g therefore forces\n\n    gamma_L=gamma:=D'/D,\n    CM+EM'=D'(1-u),\n    E=[gamma S-u(D'-M)]/(M+d).                      (12)\n\nSince E>=0, every member of this four-fan family satisfying full PSD\nat the Huber value necessarily obeys\n\n    u/gamma<=S/(D'-M)= (M-1)/(3M+2d-1).             (13)\n\nOn the other hand, evaluate the FULL parent matrix (4) at x_start=0,\ng_0=1 and every other parent gradient zero. The additional incoming\nfinite mass at index 0 is (C+E)w^L_0. Formula (6) gives its unscaled\nvalue exactly as\n\n    2w^L_0[(1+a)u-a gamma]-2a gamma in^L_0.          (14)\n\nThe child has w^L_0>0 by Section 2. Thus the parent matrix is strictly\nnegative whenever\n\n    (M-1)/(3M+2d-1) < a/(1+a).                     (15)\n\nEquivalently, (15) says (1-2a)M<1+2ad. A simple sufficient domain is\n\n    a>=2/5, d/M>=a.                                (16)\n\nIndeed 2a^2+2a-1>=3/25>0 there, so\n(1-2a)M-2ad <=(1-2a-2a^2)M<0<1.\n\nConsequently NO such Huber-tight four-fan join is possible in (16)\nwhen the left child has independent physical and proof moments.\nThe child arrays may be dense and contain all ordered finite pairs;\nthe obstruction does not result from deleting their earlier edges.\n\nThe proportional queried-weight case is expressly outside this argument.\nSo is a different cross-block edge pattern, a nonsaturated objective\nconstruction that changes the weights and multipliers, or a general\nunrestricted certificate. This is not a nonexistence theorem for H_m.\n\n## 5. An explicit recursively specified candidate, rejected at every q\n\nThere is a particularly simple fully specified instance of (9). For\nevery join put V=D'-M=3M+2d-1 and\n\n    gamma=D'/D, z=gamma(M+d)/V,\n    u=gamma-2z=gamma S/V,\n    r=gamma-z=gamma S'/V,\n    k=(3z-gamma)D=bD'/V,\n    l=0, A=z, B=0, C=2z, E=0, p0=q0=0.             (17)\n\nFor S>0 and d>=0 all of gamma,z,u,r,k are positive. Equations (7)\nand (8) hold exactly: u+r=2gamma-3z and k=(3z-gamma)D.\nThus the rule is:\n\n* scale both complete old finite arrays by gamma;\n* add 2z w^L_i on (p,i) throughout the left block;\n* add z w^R_i on (p,i) throughout the right block;\n* use proof weights (u w^L, r w^R), adding k at p.\n\nAll FINAL finite coefficients and optimum coefficients in (4) are\nnonnegative. Every value cancels; the full remainder is (9)--(10).\nUnlike Section 4, this is an explicit array at every depth, even after\nits PSD test fails, and does not require any moment-rank hypothesis.\n\nFor a one-step seed h=1+a with 0<a<=1/2, take\n\n    w=(1+2a,2), D=3+2a,\n    lambda_(0,1)=2, lambda_(1,0)=1-2a,\n    R=(1-2a)||g_0-g_1||^2.                          (18)\n\nDirect expansion gives (2) with these coefficients. This is a full\nHuber-level objective certificate. At the undeformed Silver one-step\nbenchmark a=sqrt(2)-1, all coefficients are nonnegative and\nR=(3-2sqrt(2))||g_0-g_1||^2. Thus the seed is calibrated exactly.\n\nNevertheless, (17) already fails at the undeformed three-step Silver\njoin. Its M=1+sqrt(2), d=1 and a=sqrt(2)-1 satisfy (16), so (14)\nis negative. This calibration failure is not suppressed by extending\nthe formula to deformed words.\n\nMore strongly, the recursively specified array (17)--(18) has a\nnegative full g_0 diagonal at EVERY join satisfying (16), even if the\nchild remainder has already become indefinite. To see this, (14)\nuses only the child value flow, the nonnegative finite coefficients,\nand w^L_0>0. All of those hold inductively for the actual array; PSD\nof the child is not needed. Its left weight is multiplied by u>0.\nEquations (13), (15), and (17) therefore make (14) strictly negative.\n\nIn particular q can be arbitrarily large in (4): x_start=0 makes the\nq term vanish, so changing the bound without changing the actual\nmultiplier array cannot repair it. The negative assignment is a formal\nfull-matrix test, NOT a legal interpolated function trajectory.\n\n## 6. Application to the actual horizon-dependent critical word\n\nLet lambda=1+sqrt(2), m>=64,\n\n    c=lambda(1+1/m), a=(1-1/m)/c,\n    d_j=a c^j, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j).\n\nWithin this fixed horizon, use (18) on H_1 and (17) at every later join.\nLet M_j=1+sum H_j. Directly from the word,\n\n    M_0=1, M_(j+1)=2M_j+d_j,\n    M_j=[a/(c-2)]c^j+[1-a/(c-2)]2^j.              (19)\n\nWe have 0<a<c-2, hence d_j/M_j>=a for every j: write\nM_j/c^j=A+(1-A)(2/c)^j<=1 with A=a/(c-2) in (0,1).\nAlso a>=2/5 for m>=64. The function (m-1)/(m+1) increases, and at\nm=64 this is equivalent to 63sqrt(2)>=89, whose square is the strict\ninteger inequality 7938>7921. The seed satisfies a<sqrt(2)-1<1/2.\n\nThus every join from H_1 onward satisfies (16). For every m>=64 the\nfull final matrix generated by this explicit array is negative on\nx_0=0, g_0 nonzero, all other gradients zero, for EVERY q. This is an\nactual full-matrix obstruction for the stated critical parameters,\nnot just an asymptotic scalar calculation or a matrix tested on a\nselected primal family.\n\nReplacing only the final physical step by any prescribed nonnegative\nnumber leaves this negative evaluation unchanged: its gradient is zero,\nso every substituted position is unchanged. The same array remains\nvalue-canceling. This observation excludes that repair to this array,\nnot independently chosen certificates for a modified schedule.\n\n## 7. What remains open\n\nThe new family genuinely permits proof weights different from the\nphysical steps and retains both moments in every full coefficient.\nIts failure is caused by the exact incompatibility between the\nHuber common-gradient kernel and the first-gradient diagonal.\n\nThe target requires some q_m B_(2^m-1)<1 asymptotically, not necessarily\nHuber equality. Neither the original schedules nor this weaker upper\ntarget are ruled out. Unrestricted cross-block interior edges, a\nnonsaturated rule with a different multiplier array, and proportional\nqueried-weight certificates remain possible. None has been proved to\ngive the required growing-horizon upper bound here.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"plan_id": "3153862b814bfe16", "branch_id": "max_independent_history_four_fan", "subgoal": "Scalable full objective upper certificate on actual critical words", "status": "refuted for displayed rule; target unresolved", "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "artifact": "research/max_independent_history_fan_obstruction.md", "exact_audit": "research/independent_history_exact_audit.py", "calibration": "Exact valid one-step Silver seed, rejected undeformed Silver three-step join; rational 1/3/7-step full coefficient audit passed.", "first_negative_examples": ["-40174/19475", "-20986432/2083825"], "new_solver_calls": 0, "new_verifier_calls": 0}, "glossary": {"C_ij": "Nonnegative interpolation slack -2 Q_ji; diagonal C_ii is defined as zero.", "J_w": "Independent proof-weight intrinsic objective energy defined in Section1.", "w^L": "Nonnegative left-child proof weights, distinct from the physical step vector.", "w^R": "Nonnegative right-child proof weights, distinct from the physical step vector.", "W_L": "Left proof-weighted gradient moment.", "W_R": "Right proof-weighted gradient moment.", "P_L": "Actual left-block physical gradient displacement.", "P_R": "Actual right-block physical gradient displacement.", "D'": "Parent Huber denominator 2(2S+1+d)+1.", "M'": "Parent shifted physical mass 2M+d.", "gamma_L": "Left child finite-array scaling u+C+E.", "gamma_R": "Right child finite-array scaling r+A+B.", "p0": "Nonnegative correction multiplier on C_(p,q).", "q0": "Nonnegative correction multiplier on C_(q,p).", "R_L": "Full gradient remainder of the left child's exact weighted certificate.", "R_R": "Full gradient remainder of the right child's exact weighted certificate."}}
{"id": "8d60e800f95e6026", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-26T20:09:43.011922+00:00", "author": "max4", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "Explicit finite full-objective selection, with nonzero/singular remainders allowed: ANY supplied actual certificate at ANY q admits a one-move removal of all incoming terminal edges by lambda_ij += a_i b_j/(A+2), lambda_in=0, lambda_nj=2b_j/(A+2). Full initial-distance coefficient, actual signs and every value flow are preserved. Exact complete feasible interval and Ker M(t)=Ker M_old intersect Ker d are proved; rank can grow by one. A finite extension removes all zero-step nodes. Exact positive-step and intrinsic-energy counterexamples delimit the rule. Conditional H3/C51 output coefficients are supplied without a new solve.", "evidence": "# Constructive full-objective terminal redistribution\n\nStatus: self-contained candidate, not verifier-accepted. No numerical solve\nand no fact_submit call. The accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290 already proves\nexistence of a selected tight certificate without incoming terminal edges.\nThe new object here is an EXPLICIT transformation of ANY supplied full\ncertificate, an exact feasible interval, its full kernel effect, and a\nfinite extension to zero-step queries. It is not a new existence claim\nbeing substituted for that accepted fact.\n\n## 1. Full formal setting\n\nLet n>=0, h in [0,infinity)^n, and let x0,g0,...,gn be independent formal\nvectors. Set xi=x0-sum_(t<i)h_t gt, and set the optimum jet\nx_star=g_star=f_star=0. The formal values f0,...,fn are independent.\nWrite I={star,0,...,n}, J=I minus {n}, and\n\n Qij=fi-fj+<gi,xj-xi>+||gi-gj||^2/2,  Qii=0.\n\nLet q be any real certificate level. Suppose lambda_ij>=0 is an actual\narray for which every value coefficient cancels in\n\n M_old=q||x0||^2-2fn+sum_(i!=j)lambda_ij Qij,             (1)\n\nand the resulting quadratic is PSD on the FULL original vector space.\nAll zero-step gradients remain independent. No Huber tightness,\npositive-definiteness, support restriction, or bound on the steps is\nassumed. Necessity of Qij<=0 for a legal trajectory and normalization are\navailable from accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290. The algebra below takes the\nsupplied polynomial identity (1) as its hypothesis.\n\nPut\n\n a_i=lambda_(i,n), b_i=lambda_(n,i) for i in J,\n A_in=sum_(i in J)a_i, B_out=sum_(i in J)b_i.\n\nThe fn coefficient gives B_out=A_in+2>0. For i,j in J with i!=j define\n\n lambda_new,ij=lambda_ij+a_i b_j/B_out,\n lambda_new,(i,n)=0,\n lambda_new,(n,j)=2b_j/B_out.                           (2)\n\nEvery entry in (2) is nonnegative. Diagonal products a_i b_i/B_out are\nnot added: Qii=0, and omitting a loop changes no value flow.\nThe update also works when the optimum has nonzero outgoing multipliers.\n\nFor each finite prefix index i, the old prefix-only flow is b_i-a_i.\nThe added prefix flow is a_i-A_in b_i/B_out. Their sum is 2b_i/B_out,\nexactly the new incoming flow from n. At n the outgoing flow is2 and\nthere is no incoming flow. Thus every original value coefficient cancels.\n\n## 2. Full PSD identity, including singular remainders\n\nIf A_in=0, the array is already unchanged by (2). Assume A_in>0 and put\n\n y_j=x_j-g_j, delta_j=f_j-||g_j||^2/2,\n alpha_j=b_j/B_out, ybar=sum_(j in J)alpha_j y_j,\n gbar_in=sum_(i in J)a_i g_i/A_in,\n V_out=x_n-ybar, d_vec=V_out-gbar_in, r_vec=g_n-V_out.\n\nThe optimum contributes y_star=g_star=delta_star=0. All V_out, d_vec\nand gbar_in are independent of g_n because no iterate position uses g_n.\nThis independence is essential.\n\nThe residual can be rewritten exactly as\n\n Qij=delta_i-delta_j+<gi,y_j-y_i>.\n\nTherefore, for every i,j in J, including i=j,\n\n Q_(i,n)+Q_(n,j)-Qij=<gi-gn,y_n-y_j>.\n\nSumming with a_i b_j/B_out and using B_out-A_in=2 proves the\nidentity, on ALL original formal vectors and values,\n\n M_old-M_new=A_in<g_n-gbar_in,g_n-V_out>.                (3)\n\nThe new array has terminal outgoing mass2 and no incoming edge. Its\ncomplete terminal square is\n\n M_new=||r_vec||^2+R_new,                               (4)\n\nwhere R_new is the full quadratic in (x0,g0,...,g_(n-1)) left after\nthe values cancel. To see the coefficient and center explicitly,\nthe terminal contribution is\n -2fn+2sum_j alpha_j Q_(n,j)\n =||g_n-V_out||^2-||V_out||^2-2sum_j alpha_j delta_j.\nAll remaining terms are independent of g_n.\n\nCombining (3) and (4), then completing the independent g_n square,\n\n M_old=(A_in+1)||r_vec+A_in d_vec/[2(A_in+1)]||^2+R_old,\n R_old=R_new-c_in||d_vec||^2,\n c_in=A_in^2/[4(A_in+1)].                              (5)\n\nFor every fixed choice of prefix formal vectors, g_n can set the first\nsquare in (5) to zero. Full PSD of M_old therefore implies R_old>=0\non the entire prefix space. Inserting R_new=R_old+c_in||d_vec||^2\ninto (4) gives the promised explicit full certificate:\n\n M_new=||g_n-V_out||^2+R_old+c_in||V_out-gbar_in||^2>=0. (6)\n\nThe initial-distance coefficient stays exactly q. No trajectory\nrestriction, prefix quotient, or equality between duplicated gradients\nhas been imposed. In particular this proof is valid for singular M_old,\nempty schedules, and schedules with arbitrarily large positive steps.\n\n## 3. Exact interval and kernel change of the directed move\n\nScale the entire coefficient change (2) by a real parameter t, starting\nat the old array. The actual coefficients are\n\n lambda_ij(t)=lambda_ij+t a_i b_j/B_out     (i,j in J, i!=j),\n lambda_(i,n)(t)=(1-t)a_i,\n lambda_(n,j)(t)=(1-t A_in/B_out)b_j.                   (7)\n\nFor A_in>0 their EXACT simultaneous sign interval is\n\n t_sign<=t<=1,\n t_sign=max_{i!=j in J, a_i b_j>0}[-B_out lambda_ij/(a_i b_j)],  (8)\n\nwhere the maximum of the empty collection is -infinity. The bound from\noutgoing coefficients is t<=B_out/A_in>1, so it is redundant. All\nfunction-value cancellations hold for every t, by linearity of the\nalready checked old and new flows.\n\nFor every t<=1 put a_t=A_in(1-t)>=0 and c(a)=a^2/[4(1+a)]. Directly\nfrom (3)--(5), the FULL remainder is\n\n M(t)=(1+a_t)||r_vec+a_t d_vec/[2(1+a_t)]||^2\n                           +R_new-c(a_t)||d_vec||^2.  (9)\n\nBecause g_n is independent, (9) is PSD iff\n R_new-c(a_t)||d_vec||^2>=0 on the ENTIRE prefix space.\nOn 0<=t<=1, c(a_t)<=c(A_in); its derivative is\n a(a+2)/[4(1+a)^2]>=0. Equations (5) and (9) thus prove feasibility\nof the WHOLE chosen segment, not only its endpoints.\n\nHere is a closed expression for the entire feasible interval, still\nincluding singular prefix matrices. If d_vec is identically zero,\nevery t<=1 passes PSD and (8) is the exact combined interval.\nOtherwise let Rmat_new be the scalar coefficient matrix of R_new in\nthe prefix vector list, and let d_row be the coefficient column for\nthe linear vector expression d_vec. From (5),\n Rmat_new>=c_in d_row d_row^T with c_in>0.\nEvery kernel vector of Rmat_new is therefore orthogonal to d_row,\nso d_row is in Range(Rmat_new). Define the Moore--Penrose inverse by\ninverting its strictly positive eigenvalues and setting the others to0,\nand put\n\n gamma_in=d_row^T Rmat_new^+ d_row>0,\n a_max=(2+2sqrt(1+gamma_in))/gamma_in,\n t_psd=1-a_max/A_in.                                  (10)\n\nWhiten on Range(Rmat_new). The rank-one subtraction in (9) is PSD\nexactly when c(a_t) gamma_in<=1; the kernel rows already vanish.\nSolving gamma_in a_t^2<=4(1+a_t) for a_t>=0 yields a_t<=a_max.\nConsequently the EXACT combined coefficient/PSD interval is\n\n                   max(t_sign,t_psd)<=t<=1.           (11)\n\nOld feasibility implies t_psd<=0, and nonnegative old entries imply\nt_sign<=0. In every case t=1 is legal and deletes every incoming\nterminal edge in a single directed step.\n\nFor A_in>0 and any 0<t<=1, c(A_in)-c(a_t)>0. Equation (9), rewritten\nwith R_old, therefore gives a complete kernel formula:\n\n       Ker M(t)=Ker M_old intersect Ker d_row_full^T, (12)\n\nwhere d_row_full adds a zero g_n coordinate to d_row. Indeed a zero\nin (9) forces R_old=0, d_vec=0 and r_vec=0; these are also exactly\nthe old-kernel conditions from (5) with d_vec=0. Thus the rank stays\nunchanged or increases by ONE, and this includes a genuine immediate\nescape from some singular PSD faces. No positive-definiteness is used.\n\n## 4. Explicit relation to all Huber-level grounded directions\n\nThis section is conditional on a supplied certificate at\nq=1/(2S+1), S=sum h>0. It does not assume the unverified finite C51\nobject. Set P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, hbar=(h,0), e=(1,...,1), and let A_traj\nhave diagonal1 and (A_traj)_(i,t)=h_t for t<i. Put\npi=A_traj e and D_hub=(2S-P_i)_(i=0)^n, whose entries are positive.\n\nThe scalar Huber samples xi=1-qP_i, gi=q,\nfi=qxi-q^2/2 are legal and achieve 2fn=q. All finite-source residuals\nvanish; Q_(star,i)=-q[1-q(P_i+1)]<0. Evaluating (1) therefore forces\nlambda_(star,i)=0 and M_old(1,q,...,q)^T=0. This conclusion uses the\nactual signs and full PSD of the supplied certificate.\n\nLet mu_i=lambda_(i,star), let L_fin be the finite row Laplacian, and\nK_ground=L_fin+diag(mu). The value flows give\n\n K_ground^T e=2e_n, K_ground e=mu.\n\nExpanding the residual using A_traj gives its full matrix exactly as\n\n M_full=[q,-mu^T/2; -mu/2,sym(K_ground A_traj)-e_n e_n^T]. (13)\n\nTo check (13), each finite Qij contributes\n<gi,(A_traj g)_i-(A_traj g)_j>+(||gj||^2-||gi||^2)/2.\nFinite value flow contributes -diag(2e_n-mu)/2 to the gradient matrix;\nthe star terms add sym(diag(mu)A_traj)-diag(mu)/2, so the mu\ndiagonals cancel. The initial-vector coefficient is exactly -mu.\n\nSince A_traj^T e_n=hbar+e_n, the Huber kernel in (13) is equivalent to\n\n                    K_ground D_hub=2hbar.            (14)\n\nIt follows that ALL differences U of the grounded matrices of two\nHuber-level certificates satisfy exactly the affine equations\n\n                    U^T e=0, U D_hub=0.               (15)\n\nConversely (15) preserves every value-flow and Huber-kernel equation;\nactual coefficient signs and full PSD must still be imposed. The\nlinear space has dimension n^2. It is spanned by U=u v^T with\ne^T u=0 and v^T D_hub=0, because bases for these two n-dimensional\northogonal complements represent every such matrix as E C V^T.\nFor a rank-one move t u v^T, the exact actual changes are\n\n delta lambda_ij=-t u_i v_j (finite i!=j),\n delta mu_i=t u_i(v^T e),\n delta M_full=t sym((0,u)(-v^T e,A_traj^T v)^T).       (16)\n\nThis explicitly includes optimum-flow changes. Fixed-mu directions\nadd the condition v^T e=0 and have dimension n(n-1). Any nonzero\nmu-changing update is indefinite as a full matrix: its x0 diagonal\nis0 while its x0 gradient row is nonzero, which is incompatible with\neither PSD or negative semidefiniteness. Therefore a path monotone\nin full-matrix Loewner order cannot perform this change at fixed q.\n\nThe terminal move (7) is a PARTICULAR genuine rank-one direction of\n(16), with\n\n u_i=a_i for i<n, u_n=-A_in,\n v_i=-b_i/B_out for i<n, v_n=1.\n\nHere a_star=0, so e^T u=0. The terminal row of (14) gives\nB_out S=sum_(i<n)b_i D_hub,i, hence v^T D_hub=0. Also\nv^T e=b_star/B_out and (0,u)g=A_in(gbar_in-g_n), while\n(-v^T e,A_traj^T v)(x0,g)=g_n-V_out. Thus (16) reproduces (3).\nEquations (8)--(12) supply an EXACT feasible interval and termination\nfor this chosen direction, which the spanning argument alone would\nnot provide.\n\n## 5. Zero-step internal queries and a finite selector\n\nLet j<n with h_j=0, and suppose the current full array satisfies (1).\nEvery position x_i is independent of g_j. The f_j coefficient is0,\nso the incoming/outgoing masses at j are equal; write them A_j=B_j.\nIf A_j=0, both sets of incident coefficients already vanish.\nIf A_j>0, for distinct i,k in I minus {j} set\n\n lambda_new,ik=lambda_ik+lambda_ij lambda_jk/A_j,\n\nand set EVERY coefficient into or out of j to0. All signs and every\nvalue flow are preserved. Define alpha_k=lambda_jk/A_j,\ngbar_j=sum_i lambda_ij g_i/A_j,\nV_j=x_j-sum_k alpha_k(x_k-g_k), d_j=V_j-gbar_j.\nAll these expressions are independent of g_j, using h_j=0.\n\nThe same triangle identity, derived afresh at this node, gives\n\n M_old-M_new=A_j<g_j-gbar_j,g_j-V_j>\n  =A_j||g_j-(gbar_j+V_j)/2||^2-A_j||d_j||^2/4.        (17)\n\nNow M_new itself is independent of g_j. Minimize the old full\nquadratic over that independent vector. Full PSD forces\nM_new-A_j||d_j||^2/4>=0, hence M_new>=0. The whole coefficient\nsegment between the two PSD endpoints is feasible. Its exact sign\ninterval is [max(-A_j lambda_ik/(lambda_ij lambda_jk)),1], with\nthe maximum taken over distinct i,k with positive product; t=1\nalways lies in it. A complete PSD interval for this zero-step\nsegment can also be read from\n\n M(t)=(1-t)A_j||g_j-(gbar_j+V_j)/2||^2\n                                  +M_new-(1-t)A_j||d_j||^2/4. (18)\n\nFor t<=1, if d_j is the zero linear form PSD adds no restriction.\nOtherwise M_new>=A_j d_j d_j^T/4 gives d_j in its range. With\ngamma_j=d_j^T M_new^+d_j>0 on the full list excluding g_j, the\nEXACT PSD condition is t>=1-4/(A_j gamma_j). This remains valid at\nt=1, when the g_j row and column vanish. Intersect with the sign\ninterval just stated. No positive-definite matrix is presumed.\n\nApply this move successively to every zero-step index j<n. A removed\nnode has all incident edges0, so a later product redistribution cannot\nreintroduce it: either factor needed for an incident added edge is0.\nFinally apply (2) at n. The output has no incoming terminal edges and\nno incident edge at any zero-step index. There are at most z+1 moves,\nwhere z is the number of zero steps. Throughout, q, every physical\nstep, all original formal vectors, all actual coefficient signs, and\nfull PSD are retained. This is an actual finite selection algorithm.\n\n## 6. Exact limits: a queried positive step and intrinsic energy\n\nThe zero-step hypothesis cannot be dropped. Consider the one-step\nschedule h0=1 and q=1/3. The nonzero full-objective multipliers are\n\n lambda_(0,star)=lambda_(1,star)=lambda_(1,0)=1.\n\nTheir full polynomial is\n\n M_old=(1/3)||x0-(3/2)(g0+g1)||^2+(1/4)||g0-g1||^2>=0.\n\nAll values cancel. Attempting the internal-node elimination at0\nredistributes the path 1->0->star and leaves only lambda_(1,star)=2.\nIts full polynomial, with the SAME actual x1=x0-g0, is\n\n M_bad=(1/3)||x0||^2-2<x0,g1>+2<g0,g1>+||g1||^2.\n\nAt scalar formal vectors x0=0,g0=-1,g1=1 it equals -1. The signs\nand flows remain correct, but full PSD fails. The invalid square\ncompletion would have treated x1 as independent of g0, which it is not.\nThis is a counterexample to this elimination rule at a queried step,\nnot to general re-selection or to the schedule's valid objective bound.\n\nLikewise, the intrinsic primitive-energy problem has a different\nterminal coefficient. For total sum s, its bare terminal term is\n-2s fn-s(s+1)||gn||^2. If the intrinsic incoming mass is A and flow\nis B-A=2s, its full terminal diagonal is A-s^2, rather than A+1.\nDeleting all incoming edges would leave -s^2<0 when s>0.\nAn exact illustration is h0=1, intrinsic\nV_h=2(f0-f1)-2||g1||^2, lambda01=2, lambda10=4. Its full remainder\nis ||g0-g1||^2. The analogous redistribution deletes lambda01 and\nleaves lambda10=2, giving ||g0||^2-||g1||^2, which is indefinite.\nThus this full-objective selector is not an intrinsic reversal theorem.\nThe previously recorded intrinsic reversal interface is a separate\ncandidate, not a proof premise here: let L_intr be the signed intrinsic\nrow Laplacian, hbar=(h,0), w=(h,1), and\nK_intr=L_intr+diag(hbar)-hbar e_n^T+diag(w). Let\nDelta_ij=1_(j=n-i)-1_(j=n-i-1), with an out-of-range indicator equal0.\nThat interface has actual canonical reversed entries\n4(1+s)^2 Phi_ij, with Phi=-Delta K_intr^(-1)Delta when K_intr is\ninvertible. Ordinary reversed signs require ALL off-diagonal\nPhi_ij>=0. Neither full PSD nor this terminal procedure supplies that\ncondition.\n\n## 7. A singular input with an exact nontrivial escape\n\nFor h0=1, q=1/3, take lambda01=lambda10=lambda_(1,star)=2 and\nevery other multiplier0. Then\n\n M_old=(1/3)||x0-3g1||^2,\n A_in=2, B_out=4, d_vec=(x0-3g0)/2.\n\nFormula (2) gives exactly the reduced array in Section6, which has\nM_new=(1/3)||x0-(3/2)(g0+g1)||^2+(1/4)||g0-g1||^2.\nHere R_new=||d_vec||^2/3, gamma_in=3, a_max=2, and both the sign\nand PSD lower endpoints are0. The EXACT permissible interval is\n0<=t<=1. The full matrix rank increases from1 to2 at every t>0,\nwhile all original formal vectors and the initial coefficient q stay.\nThis is a concrete singular-face escape using an optimum-flow change:\nmu changes from (0,2) to (1,1). It cannot lie on a path monotone in\nthe full-matrix Loewner order.\n\n## 8. Conditional application to the concrete C51 full-objective array\n\nThe PUBLIC unverified finite object047c257795dc59ba supplies a proposed\nseven-step H3 array with r_i7=(3123,5450,5265,4434,7434,9067,13402)/10^6.\nThis object is not a verified proof premise here. For ANY supplied\narray having those terminal entries, the transformation itself is exact:\n\n A_in=1927/40000, B_out=81927/40000,\n c_in=3713329/6708320000, 2/B_out=80000/81927.\n\nIn the public object's notation b_j=beta_j (j<7), b_star=mu_7. The\nentire output is given without a new optimization or matrix recovery by\n\n r_new,ij=r_ij+r_i7 beta_j/B_out  (i,j<7, i!=j),\n r_new,i7=0,\n mu_new,i=mu_i+r_i7 mu_7/B_out   (i<7),\n beta_new,j=2beta_j/B_out        (j<7),\n mu_new,7=2mu_7/B_out.\n\nIf its supplied full certificate is feasible, (2)--(6) directly prove\nthese exact coefficients feasible with the same initial-distance level\nand the same physical long steps. The statement is conditional on that\ncertificate, not an extrapolation of its seven-step validity to H_m.\nThe large-horizon critical-family upper certificate and the original\nuniform lower-bound/fixed-asymptotic-separation target remain unresolved.\n\n## Literature/process scope\n\nTwo targeted Matlas searches for constructive terminal/certificate\nelimination returned no applicable result. Web fallback recovered the\nstandard interpolation paper arXiv1502.05666 and unrelated/constant-step\ncertificate work, but no needed new mechanism. No external theorem is a\npremise. This branch is a direct algebraic strengthening inside the\nalready audited formal regime of fact1252b8823bb5a290; its full relevant\nproof and notation were re-read. No extra literature layer is needed\nfor the explicit positive-step counterexample in Section6, which tests\nthe exact same algebra and not a new class of mathematical objects.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "finding_ids": ["9becf2ae533df12e", "e30ecd052ecf8936", "047c257795dc59ba", "4a064f93111ac5a2"], "status": "complete_candidate_not_verified", "verification_allocation": "closed", "new_solver_calls": 0, "new_fact_submit_calls": 0}, "glossary": {"A_in": "Total incoming-terminal multiplier mass, nonnegative.", "B_out": "Total outgoing-terminal multiplier mass A_in+2 in a normalized objective certificate.", "a_i": "In this terminal-redistribution proof, lambda_(i,n); local coefficient notation only.", "b_i": "In this terminal-redistribution proof, lambda_(n,i); local coefficient notation only.", "gbar_in": "Incoming-mass weighted mean of the prefix gradients.", "V_out": "x_n minus the outgoing-mass weighted mean of prefix normals x_j-g_j.", "d_vec": "V_out-gbar_in, independent of the unqueried terminal gradient.", "r_vec": "g_n-V_out.", "c_in": "A_in^2/[4(A_in+1)], the exact added prefix-square coefficient.", "R_new": "Entire prefix quadratic after the reduced certificate's unit terminal square is removed.", "R_old": "Entire prefix Schur remainder of the original supplied full certificate.", "gamma_in": "d_row^T Rmat_new^+ d_row when d_vec is nonzero.", "D_hub": "The positive vector (2S-P_i) of Huber remaining-sum weights.", "K_ground": "Finite row Laplacian plus diag(mu), with mu_i=lambda_(i,star).", "A_traj": "Lower triangular matrix with diagonal1 and entries h_t in row i,column t for t<i."}}
{"id": "1f81b116bd1128ab", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-26T20:16:06.865177+00:00", "author": "max3", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "C53 extended joint proof: the whole mixed repeated-copy obstruction also survives ANY seed certificate of depth r_m whenever 2^(m-r_m)/m²->infinity. A concrete violated queried pair (n-4,n-1) proves the formal template is not legal and gives an actual nonnegative two-edge array direction that removes its 2D obstruction. An explicit attained THREE-boundary H2 state and full six-variable/Schur recurrence include that new edge at growing levels. Full new-array PSD/asymptotic feasibility remains OPEN.", "evidence": "# C53: a formal template defeats the entire mixed-boundary repeated-copy join\n\nStatus: complete joint proof candidate, **not verifier-accepted**. No solver\nor verifier call is allocated. The only mathematical input from the fact graph\nis `1252b8823bb5a290`, for the interpolation convention and full formal-vector\ncertificate interpretation. The construction suggested by shared finding\n`c7f10ed5dc99275b` is defined and expanded here; that finding is not a premise.\n\nThe conclusion concerns a specified recursively generated certificate family.\nThe formal vectors in this proof are NOT legal smooth-convex transcripts. In\nparticular, the conclusion below is neither a lower bound for unrestricted\nW_n nor a decision of the original exact-profile problem.\n\n## 1. The certificate family, including all its available parameters\n\nUse smoothness one and optimum jet x_star=g_star=f_star=0. For every ordered\npair of distinct samples put\n\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,\n    C_ij=-2Q_ji\n        =2(f_i-f_j)+2<g_j,x_j-x_i>-||g_i-g_j||^2.\n\nThe accepted fact implies C_ij>=0 on every legal trajectory. All identities\nbelow, however, are identities in independent formal vectors after the GD\nsubstitution; no interpolation constraints are imposed on test vectors.\n\nFix a>0,c>0 and define\n\n    d_j=a c^j,\n    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j),\n    n_j=2^j-1, S_j=sum H_j, M_j=1+S_j.\n\nThe initial block in the certificate recursion is H_1=(1+a), not H_0.\nFor each block choose real u_j,v_j, with final v_m>0, and a multiplier array\nlambda^(j) on its samples and star. Require the following star pattern:\n\n    lambda_(star,i)=h_i             for every queried i<n_j,\n    lambda_(star,n_j)=u_j,\n    lambda_(i,star)=0               for i<n_j,\n    lambda_(n_j,star)=s_j,\n    s_j=S_j+u_j-v_j.                                      (1)\n\nHere s_j is only an incoming optimum multiplier, not the stepsize sum S_j.\nAll final actual multipliers must be nonnegative if the array is to certify\nan objective bound. We will prove a necessary condition even without using\nthose signs. Assume exact cancellation of every function-value coefficient\nin the identity defining the gradient-only remainder E_j:\n\n    ||x_start||^2-||x_end-u_j g_end||^2-2v_j f_end\n            =sum lambda^(j)_ik C_ik+E_j.                  (2)\n\nPut\n\n    Z_j=(u_j-1)^2+v_j,\n    z_j=sum_(i<n_j) h_i g_i+g_end.                         (3)\n\nThe seed may be ANY one-step array satisfying (1)--(2). Its remainder need\nnot be PSD. In particular this includes the primitive one-step seed in the\nshared construction, with u_1=M_1,v_1=2M_1-1 and\nE_1=(2-(1+a)^2)||g_0-g_1||^2.\n\nHere is the permitted join. Write n=n_j,p=n,q=2n+1,S=S_j,\nd=d_j,b=1+d,M=M_j. Choose parameters A,B,P,Q with\n\n    r=1+A+B,\n    P-Q=b+(1-A)S.                                       (4)\n\nEmbed one copy of the old array on the left block and r times the same\narray on the right block. For every right queried index i=p+1,...,q-1 add\n\n    A h_i to (p,i), B h_i to (q,i),\n    -(A+B)h_i to (star,i).                               (5)\n\nAdd the following six sparse corrections:\n\n    (p,q): P,                    (q,p): Q,\n    (p,star): -s_j,              (q,star): s_(j+1)-r s_j,\n    (star,p): b-u_j,             (star,q): u_(j+1)-r u_j.  (6)\n\nNo restriction to H=ell=0, greedy choices of Z, PSD local terms, rank-one\nchild remainders, or PSD child remainders is imposed. The final objective\nmatrix must still be PSD on the full formal space. Nonnegative actual\ncoefficients, when they hold, include all the star coefficients in (1);\nrequiring A,B,P,Q>=0 is a sufficient way to preserve the other signs.\n\nThe desired result is a necessary condition for ANY such recursion. It is\ntherefore valid for every choice of its parameters that passes all actual\nsign tests, including choices depending on the target horizon.\n\n## 2. The complete local identity\n\nDefine\n\n    t=A-B,\n    H=M+d-B(M-d)-t M,\n    ell=P-A,\n    V=x_p-x_q-g_p+g_q.\n\nThe exact recursion for the entire gradient remainder is\n\n    E_(j+1)=E_j(left)+r E_j(right)\n       +t||V||^2+2<V,(H-ell)g_p+ell g_q>\n       +(Z_j-rd^2)||g_p||^2+(rZ_j-Z_(j+1))||g_q||^2.    (7)\n\nWe verify both the value and quadratic coefficients. Write x=x_p,g=g_p,\ny=x_q,e=g_q, and w=sum_(p<i<q)h_i g_i=x-bg-y. Summing (5) gives\n\n    2AS f_p+2BS f_q-AS||g||^2-BS||e||^2\n                      -2<w,A(x-g)+B(y-e)>.             (8)\n\nThis follows by expanding a summand: the coefficient of each f_i and of\neach ||g_i||^2 cancels, leaving the displayed linear and bilinear terms.\nThe value coefficient at p in the two children plus (5)--(6) is\n\n    -2v_j+2AS+2(P-Q)-2s_j-2(b-u_j)\n       =2[(A-1)S+P-Q-b]=0.\n\nThe coefficient at q is\n\n    -2r v_j+2BS-2(P-Q)+2(s_(j+1)-r s_j)\n                         -2(u_(j+1)-r u_j)=-2v_(j+1).\n\nThis uses (1),(4) and r=1+A+B. All other values have already canceled.\n\nFor completeness, the quadratic parts of the six expressions used in (6)\nare respectively\n\n    C_pq: 2<e,y-x>-||g-e||^2,\n    C_qp: 2<g,x-y>-||g-e||^2,\n    C_pstar: -||g||^2,       C_qstar: -||e||^2,\n    C_starp: 2<g,x>-||g||^2,\n    C_starq: 2<e,y>-||e||^2.\n\nSubtract their weighted sum and (8) from the new potential minus the two\nchild potentials. With D=x-y, all dependence on the separate x,y cancels.\nThe resulting coefficients, using the convention that cross coefficients\nmultiply twice the stated inner product, are\n\n    ||D||^2: t,\n    <D,g>: H-ell-t,               <D,e>: ell+t,\n    ||g||^2: Z_j-rd^2+t-2H+2ell,\n    ||e||^2: rZ_j-Z_(j+1)+t+2ell,\n    <g,e>: H-2ell-t.\n\nSubstituting V=D-g+e gives (7). Thus (7) concerns the full original\ngradient space, not only a condensed boundary form.\n\n## 3. A formal template on which all mixed terms vanish\n\nDefine nonzero signed scalars\n\n    k_1=2+a=M_1,\n    k_(j+1)=-k_j^2/d_j,       j>=1.                       (9)\n\nFor each block H_j and each vector e, there is an explicit formal gradient\nassignment, linear in e, with terminal gradient e such that\n\n    z_j=k_j e,\n    E_j=(k_j^2-Z_j)||e||^2.                              (10)\n\nCrucially this is true for EVERY parameter choice in (4)--(6). The same\ntemplate (9) works after a change of the storage variables, multiplier\nweights, or mixed boundary terms.\n\nFirst check the seed carefully. From (1)--(2), subtraction of the star\nterms gives the intrinsic identity\n\n    E_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_end)\n          +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n          +(M_j-Z_j)||g_end||^2\n          -sum_(i!=k finite)lambda^(j)_ik C_ik.          (11)\n\nTo obtain (11), use\n||x_start||^2-||x_end||^2\n =2sum h_i<g_i,x_i>-sum h_i^2||g_i||^2\nand s_j=S_j+u_j-v_j. This also verifies that no hypothesis s_j=0 is needed.\n\nAssign both seed gradients equal to e, and set the formal values\nf_i=<e,x_i>-||e||^2/2. Every finite C_ik is then zero. More generally,\nfor a constant-gradient block with prefix sums P_i,\nf_i-f_end=(S_j-P_i)||e||^2 and\n\n    2sum h_i(S_j-P_i)+sum h_i(1-h_i)=S_j(S_j+1).\n\nThe latter equality follows from\nsum_i h_i sum_(t>=i)h_t=(S_j^2+sum_i h_i^2)/2.\nEquation (11) therefore evaluates to (M_j^2-Z_j)||e||^2.\nFor H_1, z_1=(2+a)e=k_1 e, proving (10). The auxiliary values merely\nevaluate a polynomial identity; they are not claimed to be feasible jets.\n\nSuppose (10) holds at level j. In the right child use its template with\nterminal vector e. In the left child use its template with terminal vector\n\n    g=-k_j e/d_j.\n\nThe block displacement gives exactly\n\n    V=d_j g+z_right=0,\n    z_parent=z_left+d_j g+z_right=z_left=k_j g=k_(j+1)e.  (12)\n\nEvaluating the ENTIRE right side of (7), rather than discarding any child\npart, now gives\n\n    E_(j+1)\n      =(k_j^2-Z_j)||g||^2+r(k_j^2-Z_j)||e||^2\n           +(Z_j-rd_j^2)||g||^2+(rZ_j-Z_(j+1))||e||^2\n      =[(k_j^2-rd_j^2)k_j^2/d_j^2+r k_j^2-Z_(j+1)]||e||^2\n      =(k_j^4/d_j^2-Z_(j+1))||e||^2.\n\nThis is (10) at the parent. Neither E_j>=0 nor Z_j=k_j^2 was used.\nIn particular reducing Z at an earlier join does not remove the test:\nits resulting positive template energy is carried exactly into the next\ncalculation and cancels with that join's storage terms.\n\n## 4. Even changing the final initial-distance coefficient cannot repair it\n\nWrite u=u_m,v=v_m>0,k=k_m,Z=(u-1)^2+v. The full objective certificate\ngenerated by the final array, at any proposed level q_cert, is\n\n    M_cert=q_cert||x_0||^2-2f_n\n                         +sum_(i!=j) (2lambda^(m)_ji/v) Q_ij\n       =(q_cert-1/v)||x_0||^2\n                         +[||x_n-u g_n||^2+E_m]/v.       (13)\n\nThe whole formula follows from (2) and C_ij=-2Q_ji. Its actual\ninterpolation multipliers are nonnegative when the lambda array is.\n\nApply (10), keep the initial formal vector x_0 independent, and write e\nfor the terminal template vector. Since z_m=k e,\n\n    x_n-u g_n=x_0-(k+u-1)e.\n\nSet beta=k+u-1. The restriction of v M_cert to the two independent\nscalar coordinates in x_0,e has matrix\n\n                    [ q_cert v      -beta       ]\n                    [   -beta       2k beta-v   ].       (14)\n\nIndeed its second diagonal is\nbeta^2+k^2-Z=2k(k+u-1)-v. Full PSD requires (14) PSD.\nIts lower diagonal cannot be zero: PSD would then force beta=0, making\nthat diagonal -v<0. Consequently 2k beta-v>0, and its determinant gives\n\n    q_cert >= beta^2/[v(2k beta-v)] >= 1/k^2.             (15)\n\nFor the last inequality use the exact identity\n\n    k^2 beta^2-v(2k beta-v)=(v-k beta)^2>=0.\n\nBoth denominators are positive. Thus (15) holds whether E_m is PSD or\nindefinite, whether q_cert equals 1/v or is increased, and whether any\nchild storage was selected greedily. All nonlinear mixing parameters in\n(7) have vanished from the necessary bound.\n\nThis is a full-space certificate obstruction. Passing the test on legal\nsampled trajectories alone would not suffice, since the formal gradient\nassignment used in (10) is generally not a legal transcript.\n\n## 5. Quantitative growing-horizon consequence for the assigned words\n\nFix an integer m>=128, set epsilon=1/m, lambda=1+sqrt(2), and use the\nactual assignment\n\n    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=(1-epsilon)/c,\n    d_j=(1-epsilon)c^(j-1), 1<=j<=m.\n\nThe symbol d_m is used only to express the recurrence solution; H_m uses\nthe bridges d_0,...,d_(m-1). Put\n\n    R=(2+a)/[c(1-epsilon)].\n\nSolving (9) in absolute value gives\n\n    |k_j|=(1-epsilon)c^j R^(2^(j-1)),   1<=j<=m.          (16)\n\nTo check (16), divide |k_j| by c d_j. This ratio is R at j=1 and\nis squared at every next step, since d_(j+1)=c d_j.\n\nThere is a strict gap below one in R, with an explicit uniform constant:\n\n    1-R = epsilon[2-lambda^2 epsilon(1+epsilon)]\n                  /[lambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)]\n          > epsilon/4.                                  (17)\n\nFor the equality multiply by c^2(1-epsilon). Its numerator is\nlambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)\n -[lambda^2+(2lambda-1)epsilon]\n =2epsilon-lambda^2 epsilon^2(1+epsilon),\nusing lambda^2=2lambda+1. For the inequality, lambda^2<6 and\nepsilon<=1/128 imply\n\n    2-lambda^2 epsilon(1+epsilon)>7/4,\n    lambda^2(1+epsilon)^2(1-epsilon)<7.\n\nAlso R>0 from its definition. The bound 1-x<=exp(-x), proved for\n0<=x<1 by integrating the derivative of log(1-x), now yields\n\n    |k_m| < c^m exp[-2^(m-3)/m].                         (18)\n\nHere c<5/2: sqrt(2)<10/7 gives\nc<(17/7)(129/128)=2193/896<5/2. Combining (15),(18) gives\n\n    q_cert > (2/5)^(2m) exp[2^(m-2)/m] -> infinity.       (19)\n\nThe limit follows because 2^m/m^2 tends to infinity: its successive ratio\nis 2m^2/(m+1)^2>3/2 for all m>=7. In particular the exponential term in\nthe logarithm of (19) dominates the linear term 2m log(5/2).\n\nAlready for every m>=128, (18) gives |k_m|<1 and hence q_cert>1.\nFor an explicit elementary comparison, exp(1)>1+1+1/2+1/6>5/2, so\nlog(5/2)<1, while 2^(m-3)>m^2 for every m>=16 by checking m=16\nand noting that 2m^2/(m+1)^2>1 thereafter. Thus the exponent in (18),\nafter bounding c^m by exp(m), is negative.\n\nNo upper coefficient from this family can therefore approach the small\nobjective levels needed for a comparison with the exact Zhang--Jiang\nprofile. This is stronger than failure of the previously chosen H=ell=0\nrule: all the mixed terms, all retained child matrices, all earlier\nstorage choices, and arbitrary q_cert were included in the derivation.\n\n## 6. Scope and the surviving repair directions\n\nThe obstruction covers exactly the repeated-copy array (4)--(6), started\nfrom any H_1 seed with the star pattern (1). The identity requires neither\nPSD child remainders nor local PSD checks, and the final test includes x_0\nand every original gradient coordinate. It also permits nonzero incoming\nterminal optimum weights s_j and arbitrary u_j,v_j consistent with (1).\n\nIt does NOT exclude another complete interpolation array for H_m. In\nparticular C51's finite H_3 certificate uses a different unrestricted\narray, and is consistent with this result. The obstruction also does not\nautomatically cover independent internal coefficient changes at a parent,\nadditional interpolation edges or corrections outside (5)--(6), a\ndifferent star pattern, or a different seed size allowed to grow with m.\n\nThe concrete repair requirement is now an algebraic one: a useful changed\narray must act nontrivially on the template (10)--(12). Altering only the\nlocal V-dependent terms cannot do that. Nor can changing Z alone, since\nthe precise earlier template energy is propagated in (10).\n\nFor literature context, the gluing-plus-rank-one-plus-sparse architecture\nis present in Altschuler--Parrilo, arXiv:2309.16530v1, Theorem 3.4 and its\nproof in Sections 3.1--3.2. The exact paper was downloaded and read. No\nstatement from that paper is imported into this proof, and its certificate\nfor the undeformed silver schedule is not claimed to apply after the\npresent parameter deformation.\n\nAll new claims here remain candidates until verifier acceptance. The\noriginal unrestricted target remains unresolved.\n\n## 7. Changing only a fixed or moderately growing seed also fails\n\nThe one-step starting point is not essential. Permit an arbitrary seed\ncertificate for the entire block H_r, 1<=r<=m, with the same star pattern\n(1), all its finite interpolation entries free, and any gradient remainder.\nAbove that seed use the repeated-copy join (4)--(6).\n\nBy (11), the constant-gradient assignment on the seed gives\n\n    z_r=M_r e, E_r=(M_r^2-Z_r)||e||^2.\n\nStart the template with k_r=M_r and repeat (12). Every subsequent identity\nand the full-matrix lower bound (15) remain valid. The exact solution is\n\n    |k_m|=(1-epsilon)c^m R_r^(2^(m-r)),\n    R_r=M_r/(c d_r).\n\nIn fact R_r<=R_1 for EVERY r>=1 at the assigned parameters. To see this,\nthe mass recurrence M_(j+1)=2M_j+a c^j gives\n\n    M_j=A c^j+(1-A)2^j, A=a/(c-2),\n    R_j=[A+(1-A)(2/c)^j]/(1-epsilon).\n\nOne has 0<A<1: a>0,c>2, and\n\n    c(c-2)-(1-epsilon)\n       =(2lambda+3)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2>0.\n\nThus the displayed R_j is decreasing. Combining with (17) gives the uniform\nseed-size estimate\n\n    |k_m|<c^m exp[-2^(m-r)/(4m)],\n    q_cert>(2/5)^(2m) exp[2^(m-r-1)/m].                   (20)\n\nConsequently no such certificate sequence can provide a bounded useful\nupper level whenever 2^(m-r_m)/m^2 tends to infinity. This includes every\nfixed seed depth and, more strongly, every choice\n\n    r_m<=m-3 ceil(log_2 m)\n\nfor all sufficiently large m. The conclusion permits a different completely\nfree seed array at each horizon; it is not a finite-library assumption.\n\nA correction confined to a fixed H_3 or H_4 seed therefore cannot repair\nthe growing certificate. A useful modification must continue to act at\ngrowing recursion levels, or leave this repeated-copy architecture.\n\n## 8. An explicit violated pair and an actual array change outside the family\n\nThis section independently checks that the template is not a legal\nadversary, and exhibits a concrete multiplier direction that the previous\nobstruction does not cover. It does NOT assert full PSD after that change.\n\nFor the original one-step template define b0=1+a and M1=2+a. At every\none-step leaf its two gradients coincide. Moreover all leaf endpoints have\nthe same proximal point Y=x_end-g_end: (12) says the left and right child\nendpoints have equal proximal points, so induction propagates the common\nvalue to every leaf. A leaf with gradient w thus has endpoint Y+w and\nstart Y+M1 w.\n\nLook at the rightmost H_3 copy, which has terminal template vector e. Put\n\n    T=M1^2/(d_1 d_2)>0.\n\nIts left H_2 endpoint gradient is T e, while its last one-step leaf has\ngradient e. In the full word these samples are j=n_m-4 (the earlier\nendpoint) and i=n_m-1 (the last queried point). Hence\n\n    g_j=T e, x_j=Y+T e,\n    g_i=e,   x_i=Y+M1 e.\n\nAdding the two interpolation expressions eliminates values exactly:\n\n    C_ij+C_ji\n       =2<g_i-g_j,x_i-x_j>-2||g_i-g_j||^2\n       =2b0(1-T)||e||^2.                                (21)\n\nFor m>=128 the actual parameters have T>8/5. Indeed M1>2,\nd_1 d_2=lambda(1-epsilon)(1-epsilon^2)<lambda<5/2, so\nT>4/(5/2)=8/5. Thus (21) is strictly negative for e!=0.\nEvery legal smooth-convex transcript has both C_ij and C_ji nonnegative,\nindependently of the choice of function values. This gives an explicit\nviolated pair; no choice of values or optimum can turn this template into\nan actual lower witness.\n\nThere is also a concrete, sign-preserving change of the multiplier array:\nadd gamma>=0 to both lambda_ij and lambda_ji. It preserves every value\nflow and every star coefficient. The gradient remainder changes by\n\n    -gamma(C_ij+C_ji)\n      =2gamma[||g_i-g_j||^2-<g_i-g_j,x_i-x_j>].           (22)\n\nOn the template, the lower-right entry in (14) increases by\n2gamma b0(T-1). For ANY prescribed q_cert>0 and v>0, set beta=k+u-1\nas before and choose\n\n    gamma=max(0,\n       [beta^2/(q_cert v)-(2k beta-v)]/[2b0(T-1)]).        (23)\n\nThis makes that particular two-variable restriction PSD, by its Schur\ncomplement. The increment is a real nonnegative interpolation multiplier\nchange, not a projection of a negative eigenvalue or a free PSD addition.\n\nThe complete matrix remains to be controlled. In fact the update (22)\nitself is indefinite: setting only g_i nonzero gives a positive value,\nwhereas setting only g_j nonzero gives\n2gamma(1-h_j)||g_j||^2<0 when gamma>0, since h_j=1+d_2>1.\nThis explicitly prevents treating (23) as a full certificate repair.\n\nThe indices i,j are queried samples, so the change introduces no incoming\nterminal edge. The pair is already present in the unrestricted model of\naccepted fact1252b8823bb5a290; it was the recursive parameterization that\nfailed to allow this independent change. Equation (20) also shows why a\nuseful implementation cannot stop at correcting only a bounded seed.\n\nAn exact extension for further work is to add, at each growing join, both\nedges between p and q-1. Write w=g_(q-1), so x_(q-1)=x_q+b0 w. Its local\nremainder update is\n\n    2gamma<g_p-w,g_q+a w-V>,                             (24)\n\nobtained by substituting x_p-x_(q-1)=V+g_p-g_q-b0 w in (22).\nThis term depends on the last queried gradient separately from z_block and\ng_end. A boundary state that retains (z_block,g_end,g_last_query) can\nrepresent (24); the two-variable state alone does not retain that data.\nChoosing gamma and the old join parameters with full PSD at every growing\nlevel remains an OPEN construction problem, not a conclusion of this file.\n\n## 9. A concrete three-variable starting state for the surviving construction\n\nThe added pair can be represented with an exact, attained boundary state;\nthere is no need to assume an unspecified positive child remainder. The\nfollowing formulas initialize such an analytic attempt at H_2 for the\nactual parameters m>=128.\n\nWrite b0=1+a,M=1+b0,d=d_1,x=d/M,eta=x^2+2x-1, and\n\n    a0=(2-b0^2)/b0^2.\n\nThen 1<b0<sqrt(2), 3/8<x<sqrt(2)-1, and eta<0. For the upper bound on x,\nput s=sqrt(2)-1 and compute\nd-sM=-epsilon[1-2s^2/(1+epsilon)]<0. For the lower bound use\nd>=127/128 and M<5/2. Thus a0>0.\n\nStart from the seed in Section 1. At the H_1-to-H_2 join set\n\n    A=1/x, B=2+x, r=3+x+1/x, t=1/x-2-x,\n    P=(1+M eta)/x, Q=M,\n    u_2=M2=2M+d, v_2=2M2-1.\n\nAll actual multipliers are nonnegative: t>0, A,B,Q>0, and\n1+M eta>=1-35/128>0 since eta>=-7/64 and M<5/2. Both incoming\noptimum weights s_1,s_2 are zero, and the corrections (5)--(6) preserve\nthe star pattern as already checked in Section 2.\n\nFor H_2 gradients g0,g1,g2,g3 put\n\n    U=b0(g0-g1), V0=b0(g2-g3), D0=g1-g3.\n\nSubstitution in (7) gives the complete remainder\n\n    E_2=a0||U||^2+(r a0+t)||V0||^2+M^2(-eta)||D0||^2.   (25)\n\nTo check the substitution, these parameter choices give\nH=-t(M+d), ell=-tM, Z_1=M^2,Z_2=M2^2. In the variables\nV=d g1+z_right and g1,g3, the cross coefficient between V0 and D0\nis A d-M=0, and the D0 diagonal is M^2(1-2x-x^2)=M^2(-eta).\nTogether with the exact two seed remainders this gives (25).\n\nRetain the three boundary vectors (z,e,w)=(z_2,g3,g2). They are independent\ncoordinates because b0>0 allows g0 to vary z while holding g2,g3 fixed.\nThe relation is\n\n    z-M2 e=U+V0+(M+d)D0.\n\nThe SHARP attained boundary form obtained by minimizing (25) over g0,g1 is\n\n    K_2(z,e,w)\n       =A2||z-M2 e-b0(w-e)||^2+B2||w-e||^2,\n    A2=[1/a0+(1+x)^2/(-eta)]^(-1),\n    B2=(r a0+t)b0^2.                                    (26)\n\nFor exact attainment and the remaining PSD part, put c0=M^2(-eta),l=M+d.\nThe scalar/vector square identity\n\n    a0||U||^2+c0||D0||^2\n      =[a0 c0/(c0+a0 l^2)]||U+lD0||^2\n          +||a0 l U-c0 D0||^2/(c0+a0 l^2)\n\nproves (26), and the second square can be set to zero for any supplied\nboundary data. In particular (26) is not a test confined to a primal span.\n\nAt a subsequent modified join let a sharp child form be K(z,e,w). The\ncomplete six-boundary-variable form before eliminating internal variables is\n\n    K(L,g,u)+r K(R,e,w)\n      +t||d g+R||^2+2<d g+R,(H-ell)g+ell e>\n      +(Z-rd^2)||g||^2+(rZ-Znext)||e||^2\n      +2gamma<g-w,e+a w-d g-R>.                         (27)\n\nHere u is the left last-query gradient; w is the right last-query gradient,\nand the parent boundary is (z,e,w), z=L+d g+R. All earlier internal PSD\nsquares are retained, with the right ones multiplied by r>0. Replace\nL by z-d g-R and eliminate (g,u,R). Full PSD requires the eliminated\n3x3 block to be PSD, its cross columns to lie in its range, and its Schur\ncomplement in (z,e,w) to be PSD. If the eliminated block is positive\ndefinite, this is the explicit update K_next=C-B^T A_elim^(-1)B, where\nA_elim,B,C are the coefficient blocks of (27) in the indicated order.\nIf it is singular the range condition is essential and cannot be omitted.\n\nEquations (26)--(27), gamma>=0, the actual coefficient signs from (4)--(6),\nand the new balanced pair give a definite growing-horizon construction\nproblem. A sufficient target is a final v_m>=M_m, E_m>=0; (13) would then\ngive q_m=1/v_m<=1/M_m. No choice of all the parameters establishing this\ntarget is asserted here. The point of (26)--(27) is to retain the variable\non which the missing real interpolation pair acts, instead of repeating\nthe excluded two-boundary recurrence.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"branch_id": "C53_C55_cycle_continuation", "finding_ids": ["bc018be5d1eaecd6", "e3c8501149a5c47e"], "fact_ids": ["1252b8823bb5a290"], "artifact": "research/c53_full_mixed_join_obstruction.md, Sections7-9 added", "status": "complete obstruction and exact construction interface; no full upper certificate; no verifier"}, "glossary": {"R_r": "In C53 Section7, seed moment ratio M_r/(c d_r).", "T": "In C53 Section8, positive suffix-template ratio (2+a)^2/(d_1 d_2).", "gamma": "Nonnegative weight added to both directed C interpolation multipliers of the specified queried pair.", "K_2": "The exact attained three-boundary quadratic form in C53 equation26; not a performance benchmark.", "A2": "Coefficient [1/a0+(1+x)^2/(-eta)]^(-1) in C53 equation26.", "B2": "Coefficient (r a0+t)b0² in C53 equation26.", "a0": "One-step remainder coefficient (2-b0²)/b0² in C53 Section9.", "w": "In the new boundary interface, the separately retained last queried gradient."}}
{"id": "869f567eefca9076", "timestamp_utc": "2026-09-26T21:14:26.078804+00:00", "author": "max4", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "Joint all-cut continuation: exact common two-jump matrix and an ATTAINED legal-primal interpretation for all h_i>1, including actual H_m. At q=1/M, optimum-source flows obey sum_i(S-P_i)nu_i+(M/4)(sum nu_i)^2<=S. A fully explicit recursive nonnegative common array is quantitatively rejected by the retained cuts (2^(m-1),3*2^(m-2)) for every m>=128 and every objective q in its stated conversion family. The requested common-array feasibility versus collection-wide incompatibility at1/M_m is still UNRESOLVED; no full target proof.", "evidence": "# One shared array for all chronological three-block tests\n\nStatus: self-contained candidate, **not verifier accepted**. This continuation\nuses no optimization, new launch, installation, or fact_submit. The accepted\ndependency is `1252b8823bb5a290`, for smooth-convex interpolation and the\noriginal finite full-objective formulation. Every new statement below is\nproved within this document. In particular an earlier unverified cut identity\nor recursive identity is not a correctness premise.\n\nThe requested decision at q=1/M_m is NOT obtained: no feasible common array,\nand no infeasibility proof for the entire collection, is asserted. What is\nobtained is an exact attained legal-primal interpretation of the collection,\nits common two-jump matrix, a quantitative optimum-source budget, and a\nquantitative rejection of one explicitly constructed scalable common array\nfamily by two cuts already in the collection.\n\n## 1. Exact collection and notation\n\nLet n>=2, h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)) have nonnegative finite entries, and put\nS=sum h_i, M=1+S, P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, including P_n=S. Normalize L=1 and\nx_star=g_star=f_star=0. The original independent formal vectors are\n(x_0,g_0,...,g_n); x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t. For distinct sample indices\nin I={star,0,...,n}, write\n\n Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.\n\nChoose ONE finite nonnegative array lambda_ij with finite-node flow\n\n sum_j lambda_ij-sum_j lambda_ji=2*1_(i=n).                 (1)\n\nIts value-free quadratic polynomial is\n\n C_lambda=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i!=j)lambda_ij Q_ij.        (2)\n\nFor every 1<=k<l<=n, require (2) to be nonnegative whenever\n\n g_i=u for i<k, g_i=v for k<=i<l, g_i=w for i>=l,         (3)\n\nwith x_0,u,v,w independent arbitrary formal vectors. The same array is used\nfor every (k,l). This is the collection A_2. One-block and two-block tests\nare included by equality of block values. All the earlier queried-j/tail\ntests are included: for j>0 use k=j,l=j+1,u=0; for j=0 use a two-block\nspecialization. No position moment, optimum edge, or initial coordinate is\ndiscarded in (2).\n\n## 2. One common matrix, with all singular cases\n\nIntroduce chronological jumps d_k=g_k-g_(k-1), 1<=k<=n, and b=g_0. Then\ng_i=b+sum_(k<=i)d_k. This is an invertible linear coordinate change. Write\nthe matrix of (2) in coordinates (x_0,b,d_1,...,d_n) as\n\n              [ C       R ]\n A_lambda =   [ R^T     T ],                              (4)\n\nwhere C is 2 by 2, r_k is column k of R, and T is n by n. A restriction\n(3) is EXACTLY the principal block of (4) on x_0,b,d_k,d_l. Thus the entire\ncollection is equivalent to\n\n C>=0;\n r_k in Range(C) for every k;\n Z_kk>=0 and Z_kl^2<=Z_kk Z_ll for every k<l,              (5)\n Z_kl=T_kl-r_k^T C^+ r_l.\n\nHere C^+ inverts positive eigenvalues and vanishes on Ker(C). To prove the\nsingular statement, diagonalize C. A nonzero component of any r_k in its\nkernel makes the corresponding quadratic unbounded below in a free base\nvariable. Once every such component vanishes, complete the squares in the\npositive base coordinates. The remaining two-variable quadratic has matrix\n[[Z_kk,Z_kl],[Z_kl,Z_ll]], whose PSD criterion is exactly (5). This proves\nboth implications, including zero diagonal entries and C=0.\n\nThere are n(n-1)/2 coupled pair tests of ONE residual matrix Z. This is not\nindependent per-partition feasibility. It is also not full PSD of Z.\n\nHere is an explicit matrix-only distinction. In any three jump coordinates\ntake diagonal entries 1 and all off-diagonal entries -3/4, with every other\njump diagonal 1 and other off-diagonals zero; take C=I_2 and R=0. Every\ntwo-jump principal matrix is positive definite, but the vector with these\nthree jumps equal to 1 has quadratic value 3-9/2=-3/2. This exhibits the\nthree-jump correlation absent from A_2. It does NOT assert that this matrix\nis realized by a nonnegative interpolation array for a particular h.\n\n## 3. Quantitative budget for actual optimum-source edges\n\nSet mu_i=lambda_(i,star), nu_i=lambda_(star,i), and nu=sum_i nu_i.\nSumming (1) gives sum_i mu_i=2+nu. On the constant-gradient subspace\ng_i=b, direct expansion gives\n\n C = [ q                -(2+nu)/2 ]\n     [ -(2+nu)/2        1+2S+sum_i(1+P_i)nu_i ].          (6)\n\nFor the lower-right coefficient, finite edges contribute\nsum_i P_i(out_i^finite-in_i^finite)\n=2S-sum_i P_i mu_i+sum_i P_i nu_i. The finite-to-star position terms\nadd back sum_i P_i mu_i; the optimum-edge norm terms add 1+nu. The x_0\ncoupling is -(sum_i mu_i)<x_0,b>. These are exactly (6).\n\nAt the proposed q=1/M, positivity of C is equivalent to\n\n sum_i (S-P_i)nu_i + (M/4)nu^2 <= S.                     (7)\n\nIndeed its determinant is\n1-1/M-sum_i(S-P_i)nu_i/M-nu^2/4, and its first diagonal is positive.\nIn particular nu<=2sqrt(S/M), with a stricter penalty on early\noptimum-source mass. This uses the actual optimum edges, not a choice to\ndelete them. The budget alone does not prove infeasibility, since nu=0\nremains allowed.\n\n## 4. The legal primal cone represented by the collection\n\nLet V_(k,l) be the subspace of real coordinate lists\n(X,G_0,...,G_n) in which G is constant on the three blocks (3), while X is\nfree. Define the cone\n\n K_2=sum_(1<=k<l<=n) { positive semidefinite matrices with range in V_(k,l) }.\n                                                                    (8)\n\nEquivalently, K_2 consists of finite sums vv^T where each scalar gradient\nlist in v has at most two chronological jumps. This follows by diagonalizing\neach positive semidefinite summand. Conversely each such vv^T lies in one\nof the summands, after inserting an unused cut if necessary.\n\nThus G belongs to K_2 if and only if it has a Gram realization in a finite\nEuclidean space with an orthonormal coordinate system in which every\ncoordinate of (g_0,...,g_n) changes at most twice. Different coordinates may\nuse different cuts.
